Absolute Regression
===================

Chapter 151: When We're Together.
---------------------------------
The village was ordinary. No, it looked ordinary.

There were passersby and merchants, and even children running about.

At a glance, no one would ever think this place was the “village of Evil Valley.”

However, though it seemed like an ordinary village at first glance, the gazes, wariness, and atmosphere of those coming and going were anything but ordinary.

We sensed furtive glances passing between them, but pretended not to notice as we walked along the road.

“Not everyone here follows the Lord of Evil Valley. Rather, there are many who constantly covet his position. You can think of it as similar to the Green Forest.”

That was why we did not rush in using lightness skill.

We made sure our movement would not be taken as an intrusion or an attack. If it were judged to be an attack, every villain in Evil Valley would step forward. Whether they liked it or not, they would try to defend their home.

But we were merely walking as unfamiliar visitors. That difference was enormous, and it was a strategy we could choose because we had the Extreme Evil Little Demon, who understood the nature of Evil Valley better than anyone.

As we walked along the road, we saw more than ten men and women sitting around in an open space, drinking. Whatever was so pleasant and enjoyable, they were laughing and chattering noisily.

The very moment we passed them.

They sprang to their feet and tried to throw hidden weapons. But before the hidden weapons could even leave their hands—

Shiiiiik.

Kwakwakwakwakwa!

A step faster than them, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s saber qi swept them away. We had already been prepared for their ambush.

Their acting had been fine up to the point of laughing, chatting, and playing, but even after we passed by, not a single one of them had so much as glanced at us. If it had been two or three people, perhaps, but with more than ten sitting there, not one looking at us was unnatural in itself.

“Most of the villains in Evil Valley are people who would sell even their souls for money. The Lord of Evil Valley has probably put a bounty on us in preparation for our arrival.”

At the Extreme Evil Little Demon’s explanation, I said with a smile,

“He’d have to put up at least a million nyang.”

Then the Drunken Demon said,

“Per person, of course?”

We continued walking while joking like that, when ambushers sprang out again from the woods on both sides.

This time, the Sword Venerable lightly soared upward and spun once in the air, unleashing sword qi. Her sword turned with her, scattering sword qi in every direction.

Those who charged were cut down by the sword qi and all collapsed dead, and not a single strand of sword qi missed its mark.

The Sword Venerable descended once more with elegant footwork to the spot where she had been walking.

As if nothing had happened, we continued the conversation from before as we walked.

“The Lord of Evil Valley isn’t the richest man under heaven. How could he possibly put up a million nyang per person?”

“That’s why only people like this are coming out.”

The moment the Drunken Demon spoke, hidden weapons flew in from the thicket ahead.

We all lightly twisted our bodies and avoided them. The hidden weapons passed through the gaps between the five of us. They flew in fiercely, as if they could never be dodged, but everyone moved lightly and avoided them all.

This time, the Drunken Demon moved. His figure flashed, then vanished into the thicket. And he returned to his original spot even faster than when he had gone in. Not a single scream was heard, so we could not tell how he had dealt with the ambushers.

After returning, he drank a cup of wine and said,

“I gave them all a cup, so they drank and went to sleep.”

I had never properly seen the Drunken Demon fight, but just from this, he was not someone whose martial arts could be underestimated.

After walking on again, we arrived at a ferry.

“From here, we have to take a boat and cross the river.”

We boarded a boat procured at the ferry and crossed the river.

“As the youngest, I’ll row.”

Watching me row, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said,

“When did you learn how to handle a boat? You’re very used to it.”

“Is there anything I can’t do?”

The other three Demon Venerables also stared at me intently.

“Why are you all staring like that? It’s embarrassing. If you keep doing that, I’ll… row even better, you know? I happen to like being the center of attention.”

I joked, but they all looked at me with strange eyes. It was probably because there were too few things I could not do—far too few.

Like that, the boat swiftly cut through the current.

After staring at the river water for a while, the Drunken Demon suddenly said to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon,

“Seeing you make your move against the enemies this time, senior, you are still hale and vigorous.”

When the Drunken Demon suddenly praised him, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was visibly flustered.

“Well, I don’t drink much.”

Then the One Flower Sword Venerable said,

“At times like this, just say, ‘Thank you.’ Why is it that even with age, your spitefulness doesn’t lessen?”

She was interfering, but her tone was gentle. Normally, he might have become even more prickly, but this time, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon took a step back.

“Thank you.”

He had meekly thanked the Drunken Demon.

“Not at all. I only said what I felt.”

The One Flower Sword Venerable looked at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon with an expression that asked what had gotten into him.

I could feel that the Drunken Demon was making an effort. That effort was creating change.

The Drunken Demon’s gaze met mine. Thank you, brother. You’re doing well.

The attacks continued.

When the boat reached the middle of the river, ambushers burst out from under the water and launched hidden weapons.

This time, I leaped up while holding an oar and struck them down.

I struck one, stepped on him, then struck another; stepped on that one and struck again. It seemed like nothing special, but it was a technique combining the highest level of footwork and lightness skill.

All those who had leaped up were hit by the oar, their skulls bursting as they fell back into the water. Dressed in fish-scale attire, they were the Yellow River Water Ghosts who had once roamed the Yellow River region. They had plundered passing boats and slaughtered passengers, and in the end had been driven all the way to Evil Valley to live.

The very moment I landed back on the boat—

Boom!

With an explosion, the ferryboat we were riding was blown apart.

The Demon Venerables and I all soared upward. The Water Ghosts poked their heads out of the water and fired poison needles from tubes held in their mouths.

Pikpikpikpikpikpikpik!

But not a single Demon Venerable was struck by those poison needles. They moved their bodies in midair and avoided every ambush. The one to act this time was the Extreme Evil Little Demon.

Ping! Piiing! Ping! Ping!

The Blood Calamity Finger was released in succession. Only then did I learn for the first time that the Extreme Evil Little Demon’s finger wind could be fired continuously like that.

Those who had shot the poison needles floated up as corpses like dead fish.

The Demon Venerables and I each landed on pieces of the shattered boat. Though we stood on fragments smaller than a man’s forearm, they did not sink.

Shaaaaaaaa.

The pieces of wood carrying the Demon Venerables cut through the current and advanced.

There were Water Ghosts far behind us, poking their heads out of the water, but they did not dare attack us. Just the technique of crossing the river atop broken pieces of wood by exerting internal energy was enough to make them lose the will to fight.

Like that, we reached the opposite riverbank in an instant.

The ones whose names were known blocked our path about a hundred zhang away from there.

The movement arts displayed by the five martial artists standing in our way were anything but ordinary.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon recognized them.

“The Five Evils of Chongqing!”

They were men who had once committed atrocities with the Chongqing region as their base.

“It’s been so many years that few people recognize us now. Old man, who are you?”

“You’ve forgotten me.”

“Do you know me?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon merely smiled faintly.

“Seeing as you’re blocking our way, when one grows old, judgment becomes as clouded as memory.”

The First Evil’s expression stiffened.

He had stepped forward to kill, yet some inexplicable unease pricked at him. He was sure he had seen this old man somewhere, but he could not remember.

“Kill them!”

It was an order the First Evil gave to his subordinates, but in the end, it became an order given to the Demon Venerables.

No sooner had his words ended than each of us faced one opponent.

The Extreme Evil Little Demon released the Blood Calamity Finger. Unable to dodge the Blood Calamity Finger fired at close range, the Fourth Evil fell with his forehead pierced through. The One Flower Sword Venerable’s sword technique severed the Second Evil’s neck, and my Azure Heaven Form split open the Fifth Evil’s chest in the blink of an eye. The Third Evil’s head was shattered by the Drunken Demon’s Blood Tears, and he collapsed.

The only one left was the First Evil. The moment they died, he remembered.

“Ah! You… Why are you here?”

His voice trembled violently. There had once been a time when the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had shaken the jianghu. Back then, the First Evil had not even been able to meet his eyes properly, and now, after so many years, they had met again here.

The First Evil hurriedly tried to flee, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s great saber cut his body in half.

While fighting together, I was realizing one thing. Though they seemed to fight indifferently, the Demon Venerables were conscious of one another. They used each move carefully, and while pretending not to look, they attentively watched what techniques the others used. In a sense, the opponents they were paying attention to were not the villains of Evil Valley, but one another. And what they watched most carefully was, naturally, my movements and martial arts.

Even so, I did not bother hiding my skill. I did not reveal everything, but neither did I deliberately try to diminish my abilities. Because I was someone who would become their leader. Because I had to make them trust and follow me.

This time, those who used poisonous smoke attacked.

Several men who had been hiding revealed themselves and sprayed poisonous smoke at us.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable stepped forward at the same time, and when they swung their sleeves, the incoming poisonous smoke flew back toward those who had released it.

Then they collapsed in agony, blood gushing from every hole in their bodies.

For the first time since entering this place, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable coordinated to face the enemy. They looked at each other. The gazes they directed at one another were growing softer and softer.

“Once we pass that field, the Lord of Evil Valley’s residence will appear.”

With about a hundred zhang left until the Lord of Evil Valley’s residence, villains holding spears poured out all at once from one side.

The huge man at the front was holding a spear as large as a pillar.

He was the Giant Strength Ghost Spear, who had once made a name for himself in the martial world with a single spear. A master of the unorthodox path, he had attained mastery in spear arts, but after attacking a branch of the Martial Alliance, he had been branded a public enemy of the martial world and disappeared. Now he had shown himself here in Evil Valley.

Dozens of villains also appeared from the opposite side. Wearing black martial robes and masks that revealed only their eyes, they were assassins. The one leading them was Yin Yue. He had once been famed as an assassin of the highest skill, and here he was leading an organization of assassins.

Those who appeared from another direction wore only undergarments on their lower bodies. Standing there with vacant eyes, they were intoxicated by drugs.

But if one let one’s guard down because of their appearance, one would have one’s entire body torn apart and die. They were far stronger in martial arts than they appeared, and they were men who felt no pain even when stabbed by blades and swords. When such people made up their minds and charged like madmen, there were not many who could withstand their momentum.

Not only them, but this time a group made up solely of women also appeared, as did men dressed as hunters, and even a band of beggars.

Those who appeared were not people receiving the Lord of Evil Valley’s orders, but rather those who each possessed their own forces and lived here. They fought among themselves, trying to seize supremacy over Evil Valley.

It was also because of these people that, when the Lord of Evil Valley’s hands and feet died, he had judged his position to be in danger.

The killing intent and momentum directed at us were no ordinary thing, but the Demon Venerables were not shaken in the slightest.

Wearing his bloodstained mask, the Extreme Evil Little Demon gazed at them haughtily with his arms crossed. That composure was overwhelming the enemy.

The other Demon Venerables were the same. Merely by standing there proudly, they made hundreds of villains unable to dare charge.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon stepped forward and shouted in a thunderous voice,

“If you’re going to come at us, all of you come at once.”

Guuuuuuung!

Saber aura gathered on the Heaven-Extinguishing Great Saber hanging at his side.

His aura was red as blood. It was my first time seeing his saber aura, and perhaps because it was blood-red, it was truly intense and impressive.

Starting with him, the Demon Venerables each released their own presence. Sword aura also gathered on the One Flower Sword Venerable’s White Flower Sword. Her sword aura was pure white. It was a color that suited her far too well.

As the momentum of the five of us spread in every direction, the villains backed away further, as if pushed by an invisible force.

If I had come alone, or if I had come with only the Extreme Evil Little Demon, we would have had to fight all of them.

But since I had come with the four Demon Venerables, no one dared rush at us.

Hundreds had gathered, but they did not dare attack and only watched from afar. When we were together, there was nothing that could obstruct us.

In the end, they could not attack. Our momentum was one thing, but upon realizing that the place we were heading was the Lord of Evil Valley’s residence, they did not go out of their way to fight. Their greed had now turned toward something greater.

We began walking again. At the end of that road stood the manor where the Lord of Evil Valley lived.

On the signboard above the main gate was written: Mutual Aid in Evil. It meant that even villains aided one another in order to achieve evil.

“He must have received reports that we were coming, so he’ll be waiting with his own preparations.”

At my words, the Extreme Evil Little Demon smashed the signboard with the Demonic Extreme Berserk Palm. The entire place where the signboard had been attached was blown away and disappeared without a trace.

At the same time, the other Demon Venerables released palm force simultaneously. The main gate and the wall in front crumbled into powder.

“If I had come alone, perhaps it would be another matter, but with four of us here together…”

The Extreme Evil Little Demon walked ahead and added,

“If mere preparation were enough to deal with us, we would have to give up our seats as Demon Venerables, wouldn’t we?”

Chapter 152: It's All Your Fault.
---------------------------------
“What you said was true.”

Those were the first words the Valley Master of Villains’ Valley spoke to Yang Cheogi as he entered the main hall.

The Valley Master had sent men out to gather information about what had happened at the time, and had learned that, on that day, a young man and a masked figure had met at the Yellow River Inn. Yang Cheogi’s words had been true.

“Why would I lie to the Valley Master? I told you the truth.”

“Then that really means Geom Mugeuk’s martial arts were enough to contend with the Goe-ma.”

“They were.”

“Even if he is the Heavenly Demon’s own blood, it is hard to believe.”

“It was the same for me. I truly never imagined Geom Mugeuk would be such an expert.”

As if recalling what had happened that day, Yang Cheogi’s expression hardened. He did not seem to be lying, but the Valley Master still suspected him.

There was certainly the possibility that he had helped Geom Mugeuk and killed all of the Valley Master’s subordinates. No, that was more plausible than the fact that Geom Mugeuk had killed the Goe-ma.

What if it had been a plot by the Demon Cult to seize Villains’ Valley?

What if they had used Yang Cheogi to cut off all his hands and feet? What if even the Cheonmyeong Society had been created by the Demon Cult?

Having lost so many men as strong as himself in one place, it was only natural that he would harbor such suspicions.

Of course, there were parts of that theory that did not quite make sense.

‘The Demon Cult has never shown any interest in Villains’ Valley until now, so why are they suddenly targeting this valley?’

Villains’ Valley was, in the end, a headache for the Murim Alliance. Therefore, the Demon Cult had no reason to attack it. Moreover, if the Demon Cult directly operated Villains’ Valley, they would face fierce protests and backlash from the Murim Alliance and the Evil Path Alliance. And yet they would deliberately create trouble? When the villains here would not even offer them loyalty. Why on earth?

Lacking trust in Yang Cheogi, the Valley Master imagined every possible hypothesis. In any case, one thing was certain. Merely looking at Yang Cheogi irritated him.

‘If you have some other sinister scheme in mind, you will never leave Villains’ Valley alive.’

It was then. A subordinate entered and hurriedly reported.

“There are intruders. Gwapyo’s gang at the village entrance has been wiped out.”

The Valley Master felt that what was finally bound to come had arrived.

Yang Cheogi, who was beside him, quickly asked,

“How many intruders are there?”

“They say there are five.”

Yang Cheogi tilted his head.

“If it were Geom Mugeuk’s party, there should be three at most.”

“Why are you so certain there are three? Geom Mugeuk may have brought more demonic practitioners with him.”

“That may be so, but…”

At Yang Cheogi’s reaction, the Valley Master felt inwardly puzzled.

‘Is he truly not on their side? Or is he pretending not to know?’

Judging from his reaction now, it did not feel as though they had plotted this together.

At that moment, another subordinate rushed in.

“The Cheon family gang has also fallen to the intruders.”

“Already?”

The Cheon family gang possessed fairly strong martial arts and excelled at coordinated attacks. Yet they had already been defeated?

Soon after, another subordinate ran in.

“The insects have all been killed.”

This time, he was even more shocked. The “insects” he spoke of referred to Yangsu’s group who lived in the forest. Hiding in the woods and firing hidden weapons, they were truly difficult opponents to deal with.

Yang Cheogi and the Valley Master’s gazes met. Yang Cheogi nodded and said,

“It is definitely them.”

Considering whom Geom Mugeuk and the Extreme Evil Young Demon had killed as a pair, what on earth would happen if five of them had come?

Before the shock could even fade, another subordinate came running in, and this time the Valley Master asked first.

“Who fell this time?”

“At the Bridge of Good and Evil, the Yellow River Water Ghosts were defeated.”

“What? The Water Ghosts?”

The Valley Master was startled. One could not say they were stronger than anyone else, but in the water, the Water Ghosts were stronger than anyone.

Yes, they could die. But the problem was that the defenses were being pierced far too quickly. Their current speed was like someone striding forward and plucking flowers growing along the path.

At that moment, the Valley Master saw it. Yang Cheogi’s eyes were trembling. He was afraid.

“If you want to run, run even now.”

If Yang Cheogi was gone, he intended to pin all the blame on him and somehow search for a way to survive.

“What does the Valley Master intend to do?”

“I cannot leave this place anyway. The moment I flee, I will be giving those who covet my seat a pretext to kill me.”

As it was, all of his trustworthy subordinates were already dead.

Yang Cheogi was conflicted. He truly had never imagined that Geom Mugeuk would storm all the way here. Nor had he known that Villains’ Valley would be breached so easily. This was a place where anyone who did not know the way would wander near the entrance for days.

But if he ran from here now, the Valley Master would never accept him again. His feelings toward Yang Cheogi were already at their worst.

‘If I am to rise to the position of Valley Master, I must stop Geom Mugeuk’s party today. Only then will the villains here acknowledge me as the Valley Master.’

And if the Valley Master died in the process, that would be all the better.

While the two of them were in the midst of dreaming different dreams in the same bed, yet another subordinate entered.

“The Five Evils of Chongqing are all dead.”

Now the Valley Master was not even surprised. If all this was not a prank his subordinates had devised to startle him, then it was a calamity flying toward him at a terrifying speed.

Hatred toward Yang Cheogi surged up in his heart.

‘This is all because of you.’

If Yang Cheogi had not provoked the Extreme Evil Young Demon in the first place, claiming he would take revenge, none of this would have happened.

‘No matter what it takes, I will kill you.’

The Valley Master knew well that harboring such deep resentment toward someone would ultimately do him no good.

But he simply could not endure Yang Cheogi. Even if he came to regret this decision later, he vowed that he would absolutely kill Yang Cheogi.

The Valley Master said to his subordinate,

“Tell Elder Shi to exhaust their internal energy as much as possible.”

“Yes!”

The subordinate ran out.

“Elder Shi is the last line of defense. If he is broken through, only you and I remain.”

“Let us hope he drains as much of their strength as he can.”

If Elder Shi played his role properly, perhaps a way might open.

* * *

The courtyard was empty.

Looking around, I said,

“This feels rather eerie.”

As expected, my feeling was accurate.

The moment we crossed the courtyard and walked toward the building where the main hall was located.

Ssssss.

Our surroundings changed.

Before we knew it, we were standing in a new space, and hundreds of stone statues stood on all sides. They were statues in the shape of martial artists holding swords, sabers, and spears.

“It’s an illusion art!”

At my shout, the Demon Venerables grew tense. If one did not know how to break it, there was nothing more troublesome than this. This was also why the Murim Alliance had feared the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable the most.

“Everyone, be careful.”

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s warning, we stood in a circle with our backs against one another.

The stone statues began to move. Since they were stone statues, one would expect them to be extremely sluggish, but they were faster than expected. Things that ought to have been slow came charging in quickly, making them feel utterly grotesque and terrifying.

Boom!

The battle began with the Extreme Evil Young Demon’s Demonic Extreme Rampage Palm smashing a stone statue that had rushed toward us.

Stone statues were sliced apart by the One Flower Sword Venerable’s sword energy. To conserve internal energy, she could not strike stone blocks directly with her sword. Since it was a famed sword, she could handle a few statues that way, but if she faced this many, the sword would be damaged. So the One Flower Sword Venerable had no choice but to use sword energy.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was in a similar situation. Since he used a great saber, it was better than a sword, but even so, he could not batter stone statues apart with the precious Heaven-Destroying Great Saber. In the end, he also stirred up saber energy and saber wind to shatter the statues.

The Extreme Evil Young Demon was the same. With the Blood Devouring Finger, the stone statues continued moving even with holes pierced through them, so he had to use the Demonic Extreme Rampage Palm, which consumed more internal energy, to smash them apart.

The Drunken Demon also could not strike them with Blood Tears, so he used palm techniques to face them.

They were opponents that consumed an enormous amount of internal energy, and they surged in endlessly.

Since we could not simply waste our energy like this, I dodged the stone statues’ attacks while examining my surroundings. With the Blood-Eye Essence implanted in my eyes and the Divine Eye Art bestowed upon me, I would surely be able to see the method of breaking it.

But nowhere in this space could I see the bluish light that marked the method of breaking the technique.

‘It’s there. It definitely exists.’

Because an illusion art without a method to break it absolutely did not exist.

Using Shadow Step and Flash Step, I swiftly wove between the stone statues and began searching for the method to break it.

Then, at some point while wandering among the statues.

‘I found it!’

On the back of a stone statue farther behind, a bluish light was glowing.

It had been among the hundreds of attacking stone statues, so this was truly an illusion art that had hidden its method of breaking extremely well.

Riiiiiip!

Without hesitation, I tore through the bluish light with the Black Demon Sword and split it in half.

Beyond that light, an old man was staring in astonishment.

Puk!

The moment my sword drove into the old man’s heart, the illusion art he had cast shattered.

Freed from the illusion, we were standing in the courtyard we had entered earlier. Seeing the old man impaled by my sword collapse, the Demon Venerables realized that I had found the method to break the illusion.

The Drunken Demon made a fuss, saying, “Little Brother, you’re the best,” and the One Flower Sword Venerable looked greatly surprised.

On the other hand, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, knowing that I had killed the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, was not particularly surprised.

Lastly, the Extreme Evil Young Demon looked at me, smiled meaningfully, and said,

“Second Young Master has reached the realm of being invulnerable to all four arts.”

Since the other Demon Venerables were present, he did not mention the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, but if it had been just the two of us, he would have said this: Shameless Second Young Master, will you still claim you did not kill the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable?

I strode toward the building.

“Now then, let us go in.”

Smashing the door of the main hall, I entered with the Demon Venerables.

* * *

Boooom!

Seeing those who entered by smashing the door of the main hall, the Valley Master thought the Heavenly Demon himself had invaded.

Otherwise, how could Elder Shi’s illusion art have been broken through so quickly? Even he himself, if caught in Elder Shi’s illusion art, would have to endure until Elder Shi’s internal energy ran dry before escaping.

But the one who had invaded was not the Heavenly Demon. As if proving Yang Cheogi’s words true, the young man Geom Mugeuk walked in at the front.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Yang Cheogi and said,

“In the current situation, the only place where you could safely hide would probably be the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Ah, that will not do either. You laid hands on us, after all.”

Yang Cheogi made no answer.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned to the Valley Master.

“Are you the Valley Master of Villains’ Valley?”

“That is right. I am the Valley Master.”

“I am Geom Mugeuk, Second Young Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

“I have heard much of your reputation.”

The Valley Master tried hard to appear calm, but the pressure of the god of death walking toward him one step at a time was burning him up inside.

The Valley Master’s gaze shifted to the four Demon Venerables standing behind Geom Mugeuk.

“Honored figures of the Divine Cult have come. Welcome.”

As if treating invited guests, the Valley Master brazenly cupped his fists and greeted them.

But the four Demon Venerables merely stared at him coldly.

The Valley Master spoke gently to Geom Mugeuk again.

“I heard the madmen of this valley were greatly discourteous to the Second Young Master.”

“They tried to suck away my internal energy and crush my skull with iron chains. If you describe that as mere discourtesy, then I, too, shall be a bit discourteous today.”

“You are one who will become the supreme ruler of murim in the future. Please do not behave the same way as the pathetic scum of this valley.”

The Valley Master did everything he could to curry favor with Geom Mugeuk.

With Geom Mugeuk and the Demon Venerables standing in a line right before his eyes, he could not even summon the will to fight. If the dead Goe-ma, the Iron Chain Master, the Three Lords of the Bloody Road, the Blood Wolf Saber, the Paradise Enchantress, and the Yama Divine Lord were all here, then perhaps. But for him and Yang Cheogi alone to face the five before them was impossible.

Geom Mugeuk stared at the Valley Master and asked,

“Why did you do it?”

Then the Valley Master looked at Yang Cheogi, as if to say it had never been his intention, and asked the same thing.

“Why did you do it?”

Yang Cheogi looked at him with an expression of disbelief.

“What are you trying to pull?”

“You caused this, so you explain it.”

The Valley Master’s struggle to survive began first with distancing himself from Yang Cheogi.

When Geom Mugeuk’s party had entered, Yang Cheogi’s reaction had clearly shown that he was not one of them. In that case, there was only one choice for the Valley Master. He had to abandon Yang Cheogi. They had come to kill Yang Cheogi.

Yang Cheogi snorted.

“Do you think they will spare you just because you play such tricks?”

Geom Mugeuk answered Yang Cheogi’s words.

“I will spare you. I know full well that this entire affair was the doing of Yang Cheogi over there. If the Valley Master will only cooperate in capturing the leader of the Heavenly Mandate Society, no harm will come to you.”

The Valley Master of Villains’ Valley felt his heart pound at the hope that he might live.

‘So these people really weren’t on the same side!’

Seeing the Valley Master waver, Yang Cheogi shouted.

“Do not believe him! It is a trick to divide us!”

Unlike the frantic Yang Cheogi, Geom Mugeuk remained calm.

“We can kill both of you, so why would we bother dividing you? Four of our cult’s Eight Demon Venerables have come in person. Does this look like a game to you?”

The Valley Master did not hesitate.

“I will help. I know a clue that can lead you to the leader of the Heavenly Mandate Society!”

If cooperation meant his life would be spared, what was there to think about?

Yang Cheogi, realizing that the man’s mind had already turned, felt his heart sink coldly.

The moment the Valley Master turned against him, he knew that he would never leave this place alive.

“You pathetic, foolish bastard! This is why you’ll never escape this filthy hole!”

“Shut your mouth! This all happened because of you. I don’t even want to hear your excuses, so just go quietly to hell!”

“Crippled fool,” Yang Cheogi spat coldly, then let out a sigh. Even if he offered up information about the leader of the Heavenly Mandate Society, after trying to kill Geom Mugeuk and the Extreme Evil Little Demon, he would have little chance of surviving anyway.

‘So in the end, I’m using this.’

Yang Cheogi drew up his internal energy for the final secret technique he had never once used—no, had never been able to use.

When the Extreme Evil Little Demon saw a red aura gathering in his eyes, he issued a warning.

“Everyone, be careful! That is the forbidden secret technique of our sect’s martial arts—the Mutual Destruction Art!”

Just as the Extreme Evil Little Demon said, the martial art Yang Cheogi was about to use was the Heavenly Light Instant-Kill Art (天光卽殺術), a Mutual Destruction Art that took the user’s life along with his opponent’s. It was a final secret technique that would, without fail, kill one person and die with them.

“If your internal energy is inferior to his, you absolutely cannot block it!”

Yang Cheogi, whose internal energy was second to none, swept his gaze over each and every person there.

Whom would he kill and take with him? The Extreme Evil Little Demon, the source of this entire disaster? The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who had appeared late that day and ruined everything? Geom Mugeuk, who had made his revenge fail in the first place? Or that crippled fool, the Valley Master of Villains’ Valley?

Truly, if he could, he wanted to take all four of them with him. But the Heavenly Light Instant-Kill Art could be used on only one person.

Yang Cheogi’s eyes reddened as if they might burst at any moment, and he shouted in a ringing voice.

“Who will go with me!”

At that moment, as a heavy silence fell—

Geom Mugeuk shot his hand into the air.

“Me!”

Chapter 153: I Had No Intention of Taking You Along.
----------------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk deliberately provoked him.

Geugak Soma had said that if one lacked sufficient inner force, there was no avoiding the Mutual Destruction Art. In other words, even the Demon Supremes could be in danger. The Lord of Villains’ Valley also had to live, since they needed to hear his clues about the leader of the Heavenly Mandate Society.

But when it came to inner force, Geom Mugeuk was more confident than anyone here. No matter how profound Yang Cheogi’s inner force was, it could not surpass his own, which had already exceeded the Demon Supremes’ and now included the power he had absorbed from the Blissful Enchantress.

“Let’s go together, then, down that road to the Yellow Springs. I talk a lot, so at the very least, you won’t be bored.”

Yang Cheogi’s eyes, taking it as mockery, grew even redder. It was a hair-trigger moment, as if the Heavenly Light Instant-Kill Art might be unleashed at any second, yet Geom Mugeuk provoked him further.

“You really are leaving after dreaming only of things you’ll never achieve until the day you die.”

Those words were like touching Yang Cheogi’s reverse scale. Demon Supreme, Sect Master, Alliance Leader, even Valley Lord—he had achieved none of them, and the wound left by such a life was far too deep.

Just as Yang Cheogi decided, Yes, it will be you—

Whoooosh!

The Lord of Villains’ Valley, who had been waiting for his chance, sent a palm strike flying toward Yang Cheogi’s back.

From the Lord of Villains’ Valley’s perspective, Geom Mugeuk absolutely could not be allowed to die. If Geom Mugeuk died, there was no way the Demon Supremes would let him live.

Yang Cheogi spun around like a thunderbolt and thrust out a palm strike.

With an ear-splitting boom, both the Lord of Villains’ Valley and Yang Cheogi were shoved backward. Yang Cheogi was pushed back farther. It was not because his inner force was lacking, but because he had already drawn up his power to use the Heavenly Light Instant-Kill Art and thus could not fully display his usual strength.

The enraged Yang Cheogi shot out the Blood-Soaring Finger.

Piiing!

And the sound that followed was not the sound of the Blood-Soaring Finger piercing flesh, but the sound of a sword penetrating a body.

Thuk!

The Blood-Soaring Finger missed the Lord of Villains’ Valley’s face by a wide margin. Blood had splattered on the tip of Yang Cheogi’s outstretched finger.

He slowly lowered his head and looked down at his own chest. A blue-edged sword blade had emerged from his back and protruded out through the front of his chest.

With the sword still lodged in him, Yang Cheogi slowly turned around.

He had thought Geom Mugeuk or one of the Demon Supremes had ambushed him, but they were standing where they had been all along.

He saw the unfamiliar man who had launched the surprise attack. He was the Lord of Villains’ Valley’s last remaining limb, a master of stealth and swordsmanship. The Lord of Villains’ Valley’s final move had not been Seok No, but this man. The hidden ace he had kept secret even from Yang Cheogi had worked perfectly.

Yang Cheogi’s knees slowly buckled. He had neither the strength nor the chance to turn his head and choose someone else.

“Damn it… I had no intention… of taking you… with me.”

At that moment, red light burst from Yang Cheogi’s eyes.

Pop! Pop!

The two eyes of the man who had ambushed him burst, and he collapsed backward. At the same time, Yang Cheogi, who had poured out that light, also had both eyes burst and died on the spot.

If he had not done even this much, he would have died without ever using the Heavenly Light Instant-Kill Art, so he had no choice but to drag down the man who had ambushed him. An opponent whose name he did not even know.

He died without being able to use even his final secret art on the person he wanted. Just as Geom Mugeuk had said, even until his final moment, he had only dreamed of things he could never achieve before departing.

Geugak Soma gazed at his corpse impassively. It was the moment the long-standing grudge that had dragged on for so long ended with his martial uncle’s death, yet he revealed no emotion. He did not feel relieved, nor did he feel sorrow.

The Lord of Villains’ Valley, on the other hand, was smiling.

In the end, the one who had survived was himself. Though even his hidden trump card, his subordinate, had died, what mattered was that he had survived this moment.

“Spare me as promised!”

Yang Cheogi’s death, his subordinate’s death—none of it had any meaning to him.

Geom Mugeuk stared coldly at him and said,

“Whether you live or die depends on you.”

The Lord of Villains’ Valley began to speak about the leader of the Heavenly Mandate Society.

“The leader of the Heavenly Mandate Society never revealed his identity. Even when he met us, he appeared wearing a bamboo hat with a veil attached. If we contacted him, he would not meet us. He was always the one who contacted us and set the time and place.”

“How did the leader of the Heavenly Mandate Society draw you in?”

“What else? Money.”

He had offered an enormous sum, enough to move him.

“Still, don’t look down on me. Money is precisely what this position is. You people wouldn’t know. How unstable this seat is. How many madmen there are who would gladly die after sitting in it just once.”

What allowed him to protect that position was money.

The Strange Demon and the Iron Chain Master were both experts who could aim for the position of Valley Lord at any time. What bound their ambitions was also money, which allowed them to indulge in pleasure.

“You said you had a clue that could help find him.”

“That’s right. In exchange, promise me here and now. That you will spare me. Swear it on your Divine Cult.”

“I swear on my cult. If you give me a clue that can lead us to the leader of the Heavenly Mandate Society, I will spare you.”

Only then did the Lord of Villains’ Valley reveal the clue.

“You think I blindly trusted a man calling himself the leader of the Heavenly Mandate Society? I investigated him in secret. But it was not easy. Those I sent to investigate either disappeared or were found as corpses. Even so, I sent more, and then more. And in the end, I learned one thing.”

An unexpected phrase came from the Lord of Villains’ Valley’s mouth.

“The Little Dragon Tournament.”

The Little Dragon Tournament was a martial arts competition hosted by the Martial Alliance, selecting talented youths from the righteous sects and bringing them into the Martial Alliance’s elite.

“This is information my spies obtained at the cost of their lives. The leader of the Heavenly Mandate Society is definitely plotting something related to this Little Dragon Tournament!”

The Lord of Villains’ Valley trembled with anxiety, afraid they would say this was not enough.

But there was no need. Geom Mugeuk was recalling one fact related to the Little Dragon Tournament.

‘Ah! Come to think of it, this was that year!’

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“Fine. I’ll acknowledge it as useful information.”

“Then you’ll spare me?”

“I will keep my promise. I sincerely hope you survive.”

“?”

When Geom Mugeuk and the Demon Supremes turned their backs and left the hall, villains were waiting outside.

There was the spear-wielding Demonic-Strength Giant Spear and his men, of course, as well as Silver Moon and his assassins, groups made up solely of drug-addled men and women, hunters, and beggars. They were the very ones who had not attacked us earlier.

The moment we came out, they began pouring into the hall one by one, their eyes filled with killing intent and greed.

Curses erupted from within, and soon sounds of fighting followed. Along with the screams of several people, the scream of the Lord of Villains’ Valley could also be heard.

This sight made it abundantly clear why Villains’ Valley could not become a single organization and emerge into the world. This place was hell.

* * *

At the entrance to Villains’ Valley, I formally expressed my gratitude to the Demon Supremes.

“Thank you for helping me.”

At that, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said,

“Did we do anything? They fought among themselves and died.”

“It happened that way because all of you were here. Imagine if I had gone in alone. I’d be in there covered in blood, causing a complete uproar right now.”

My eyes met each of the Demon Supremes’ in turn.

The eyes within Geugak Soma’s eyeholes conveyed deep gratitude.

Originally, this was something that would never have happened. But because I intervened, he was able to end an old grudge.

The Single Blossom Sword Supreme smiled and said,

“Thanks to you, it was nice to get some fresh air.”

The Single Blossom Sword Supreme, who had said she would not become my wing, had served splendidly as one this time. Sometimes, the more someone says, I will not belong anywhere, the more easily they are drawn into that very place. Perhaps it is because they know they fall in deeper than anyone else that they guard against it in advance.

Beside her, the Drunken Demon drank wine. He had begun to act in earnest to reconcile the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Single Blossom Sword Supreme. Though he had made a mistake in his youth, he was now acknowledging that mistake and trying to set it right. I like that, brother.

Lastly, my eyes met the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s. If he had not come to the Yellow River Inn earlier, I might have died.

I did not say thank you. My feelings had already been carried in my gaze.

I do not know how things with these people will end. Whether things will be good like this, only for our relationship to sour over some incident, or whether we will become far closer than I can even imagine now.

I know what will happen at the Little Dragon Tournament, but my relationships with these newly connected people had never existed in the past. All I can do is give my best in each moment.

“What are you planning to do now?”

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s question, I answered without hesitation.

“I have to go to the Martial Alliance.”

The Little Dragon Tournament will be held at the Martial Alliance’s main headquarters in about two months.

“It will be dangerous.”

“This successor trial is about capturing the leader of the Heavenly Mandate Society. Failing the trial would be more dangerous for me.”

“So? You intend to participate in the Little Dragon Tournament yourself?”

“No. If I compete, I’ll be exposed in the end, so I should remain nearby. There is someone else I can have participate. Someone completely unknown in the murim.”

“Who is that?”

At that, an unexpected person came from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“Your disciple, sir.”

Looking at the startled Blood Heaven Blade Demon, Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“I’ll have Investigator Seo participate in this tournament.”

If I ask Tongcheon Pavilion, they will create a perfect fake sect for me. Seo Daeryong will compete as a member of that sect.

“What role will you take?”

“If I serve as a page or a guard, I won’t stand out.”

The Demon Supremes all sent me looks that said, Would you really not stand out just because you do that?

Perhaps worried, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon tried to come along.

“I will go too.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber.

“Everyone will think that saber is a fake, right? There’s no way the Blood Heaven Blade Demon would come all the way to the Martial Alliance carrying even the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber. Isn’t that so?”

He had phrased it indirectly, but he was saying the Blood Heaven Blade Demon would stand out too much and could not come.

Geugak Soma’s mask and the Single Blossom Sword Supreme’s Hundred Flowers Sword, engraved with red camellias, were also far too well known in the murim.

If Ian went, she would draw every gaze, and the same was true of large Jangho’s facial scar. Seo Daeryong was perfect for this task.

“This sounds fun. Little brother, I want to go too.”

As if the Drunken Demon would not stand out anywhere.

“Later, while we drink, I’ll tell you everything that happened. Ah, and regarding the leader of the Heavenly Mandate Society, I may ask you elders for help again. I still have two chances left out of the three to request aid. I’ll be counting on you then as well.”

The Demon Supremes nodded. Geom Mugeuk felt reassured. If they helped him, he truly felt confident he could even have a bout with the Martial Alliance.

‘Now then, right arm. Let’s make your name known at the Martial Alliance.’

* * *

After parting with the Demon Supremes, I headed straight for Wuhan in Hubei Province, where the Martial Alliance was located.

Using Swift Step, I raced along, savoring my utmost speed. Between trees, through crowds, leaping over boulders, I ran and ran.

No one recognized me. Even a beast that chased after me gave up, unable to catch me. In a flash, I had vanished far into the distance.

The earlier I arrived, the more time I would gain for martial training, so I ran and ran with the fastest Swift Step I could muster.

After arriving near the Martial Alliance’s main headquarters, I bought preserved food and supplies needed for living in the mountains from the marketplace, then entered the mountains.

Seo Daeryong, who had received my message, would take quite some time to arrive here, and the Little Dragon Tournament was even farther off than that.

Until then, I intended to shut myself quietly in the mountains and do nothing but train.

From that day on, I entered training.

I forgot people, forgot the mission, forgot revenge.

I trained as though I had been born solely to practice martial arts. I trained with the mindset that sleep was something to be done after death. I ate and trained, circulated my energy and trained, trained and trained.

One month later, I was walking through the marketplace as a bearded man.

I had deliberately not cut the beard I had grown in the meantime. I threw away the martial uniform I had worn during training and bought a set of shabby, cheap clothes that would not stand out.

When I went to the inn where we had arranged to meet, Seo Daeryong was waiting.

Having entered the Martial Alliance’s territory, he was visibly nervous.

Seeing him after so long made me very glad. Even though our eyes briefly met, he did not recognize me.

Then, when I sat down in front of him, he belatedly recognized me and was startled.

“Pavil—!”

Before the word Pavilion Lord could come out, I covered Seo Daeryong’s mouth.

“You want to spread rumors that I’m here?”

“No, of course not. I was just surprised.”

My unkempt hair, my beard like a mountain bandit’s. If Seo Daeryong had not recognized me at a glance, then there would be no one who would recognize me.

“But why do you look like this?”

“It’s a disguise.”

“Ah! What is with that beard? What kind of low-level disguise is this? People will stare even more.”

He said that, but he was just as conspicuous. He was wearing a white martial uniform with the character for righteousness, 正, embroidered on the chest.

“Look at me. With this much, anyone would see me as a righteous sect member, right?”

“Look around. See if anyone else is wearing clothes like that. That’s exactly the kind of outfit a new recruit who just joined the Martial Alliance wears excitedly for a few days before shoving it into a corner.”

“I still think it’s better than that filthy beard.”

“Not shabby, but filthy?”

We looked at each other and laughed together. After such a happy reunion spent attacking each other’s appearances, Seo Daeryong asked seriously,

“But why did you call me all the way here?”

Seo Daeryong had come here without hearing anything in advance. To Seo Daeryong, who was tense and on edge, I spoke with a meaningful smile.

“Let’s do a job together.”

Chapter 154: Even So, I Used to Be Number One.
----------------------------------------------
“What is it?”

Even now, Seo Daeryong had no idea what was about to happen.

“You have to enter the Little Dragon Tournament.”

At those words, Seo Daeryong was startled. He was so shocked that I almost felt sorry for him. In a trembling voice, he asked,

“You don’t mean the Little Dragon Tournament I know about, do you?”

“What Little Dragon Tournament do you know?”

“It’s the martial arts tournament held by the Martial Alliance for the younger generation of the orthodox sects, isn’t it? The one so dangerous that, with the Martial Alliance and countless orthodox experts gathering there, people from unorthodox sects—or our church—aren’t allowed to participate, or even watch. That one, right?”

“That sounds like the tournament.”

Seo Daeryong stared at me blankly, then abruptly rose from his seat and walked out of the inn without even looking back.

I did not stop him. Instead, I ordered some light snacks and wine from the waiter. I had been so immersed in training in the mountains that it had been ten million years since I’d last had a drink.

After I had drunk two or three cups, Seo Daeryong returned to the table.

“You’re not going to stop me?”

“Why would I? You were coming back anyway.”

Seo Daeryong poured himself a cup and gulped it down.

“No. This can’t be. What in the world are you talking about?”

“There’s no one but you.”

“That’s something evil leaders say when they want to work their subordinates to the bone. ‘There’s no one but you’? What do you mean, there’s no one but me? There are plenty of people if you look! If you search carefully, there might even be someone in this inn!”

Seo Daeryong thought of the people around me whom he could send in his place, then lamented that there was nothing but strange people surrounding me.

“Even so, I can’t.”

He emptied another cup.

“You do know who I am, don’t you?”

“I do. You’re a power-oriented, cold-hearted man, but also a real man who loves only one woman. When everyone else kept silent…”

“Stop!”

I omitted everything I had already said and added only the newly relevant part.

“…you’re a coward who ultimately never managed to fight Yeobin, but you’re still my right-hand man, Investigator Seo. Now I suppose I can add that you bravely entered the Little Dragon Tournament.”

“No, but how am I supposed to enter the Little Dragon Tournament?”

“It’s not as if I can enter, is it?”

“No one will recognize you. With that beard, you’re completely unrecognizable!”

“Wasn’t it a low-grade disguise? And wasn’t it a filthy beard?”

He sighed, then found another way out. Lowering his voice, Seo Daeryong whispered,

“I haven’t properly inherited my master’s martial arts yet.”

“That makes it even better. You can’t stand out. You’ll have to fight using only the basics anyway.”

Someone of Seo Daeryong’s caliber would already know how to conceal his demonic energy, so his martial arts would not give him away.

As Seo Daeryong bowed his head and resisted by radiating an aura of darkness, I brought out my strongest weapon.

“Fine. If you can’t do it, then it can’t be helped. I’ll have no choice but to fail the test my father gave me. In the end, my elder brother will become the heir. I’ll probably be assassinated and abandoned in some nameless field, where I’ll become food for beasts. Ah! But I won’t blame you, even then. I mean it.”

“I’ll probably die on the dueling platform after being stabbed by the sword of some orthodox bastard I’ve never seen in my life. Then my corpse will be sent to one of our church’s hidden sects, the one that provided my false identity. ‘Who is this?’ ‘I don’t know either.’ I suppose I’ll be some poor fool who was used to the fullest and then killed. He’s short and looks depressed. What a pity. Take him away and bury him. Ah! But I won’t blame the Pavilion Master either. I truly won’t.”

“When I become the Church Master someday, I’ll look back on today and say, ‘Ah, if it hadn’t been for you then, I could never have become the Church Master. Thank you.’”

“Someday, I’ll sit my grandchildren on my knees and look back on today as well. I’ll tell them, ‘It’s because I stubbornly refused to enter that tournament to the very end that all of you exist.’”

It was a battle between spear and shield. But no matter how fiercely he resisted, the outcome had already been decided.

“Oh, honestly, I’m going to die because of you. Then again, who can I blame? I was the one who wanted revenge for my senior when you first came to the Yellow Springs Pavilion, and I was the one who decided to learn saber techniques.”

I poured more wine into his cup.

“Thank you.”

“If you understand, that’s enough. I suppose life is about doing things you don’t want to do as well.”

“Anyone who says things like that probably lives doing only what they want.”

“No! That’s what I’m supposed to say!”

I knew this was a major decision for him. He hated stepping out in front of others more than anyone.

“As a martial artist, this will be a good experience for you too. Even the name makes it seem like fate, doesn’t it? The Little Dragon Tournament and Seo Daeryong. The Great Dragon will ascend to Heaven at the Little Dragon Tournament. If the tournament had been called the Little Tiger Tournament, I would have called Jangho.”

In the end, Seo Daeryong laughed at my foolish joke.

“Then this is an heir’s test.”

“Exactly.”

“What precisely are we supposed to do?”

“There’s a man called the Lord of the Heavenly Mandate Society. He’ll use this tournament to carry out a conspiracy. He may approach the participants, which is why you’re entering. Our goal is to catch him.”

“Of course we have to catch him. Catching bad people is what we do.”

“Just like my right-hand man.”

Once Seo Daeryong made a decision, he never looked back. That was probably why I liked him. Behind that small, gloomy-looking figure was an unyielding determination.

I took some documents from my robes and handed them to him.

“Here. This is your new identity. Memorize every last detail. You’re good at memorizing, so I’m not worried.”

“What about you, Pavilion Master?”

“I’ll serve as your servant and bodyguard.”

A flash of delight crossed Seo Daeryong’s face. He struggled to keep the corners of his mouth from rising.

“Why are you happy?”

“Me? There’s no way I am.”

“You just smiled.”

“You saw wrong.”

“You smiled.”

“Good heavens, you rascal. The elder says he didn’t, so why are you acting so mischievous? Ah, I was just practicing.”

I laughed pleasantly at Seo Daeryong’s teasing.

“For today, memorize that. Tomorrow, let’s go register for the Little Dragon Tournament.”

We took a large guest room with two beds.

As soon as we entered the room, I stopped Seo Daeryong from dusting off my bedding.

“Don’t worry about me. Think only of yourself.”

“Can you really do that?”

“I’m casually giving you an order that could change your entire life, so you’re allowed to do at least that much. You can even urinate on my blankets.”

“Receiving such orders is my life. Please don’t think of it as something bad. I’ve never thought of it that way myself. Especially not after meeting you, Pavilion Master.”

It was comforting to hear him say that.

After roughly unpacking, Seo Daeryong sat by the window and stared outside. Then he said worriedly,

“I’ll have to climb as high as possible to find out what the Lord of the Heavenly Mandate Society is plotting. I’m worried I’ll be eliminated too early.”

I did not know precisely how strong the Little Dragon Tournament was either, so I could not predict the outcome.

“Don’t worry. Old Man Doma left behind a strategy for you.”

“What? What do you mean?”

I took a small pouch from my robes.

“It’s a strategy pouch he gave me and told me to open when you reached your limit.”

Seo Daeryong was startled. He was even more shocked than when I had told him he had to enter the Little Dragon Tournament.

“Your master really gave you that?”

To be honest, I was surprised too. I never expected the Blood Heaven Saber Demon to leave something like this behind.

“Did you happen to see what was written on it?”

“No. Why? Should I open it in advance?”

“Absolutely not! If Master said to open it in a crisis, then you must open it during a crisis!”

Seo Daeryong must have been curious about what was inside, but he was deeply moved by the fact that the Blood Heaven Saber Demon had given it to him for his sake. He seemed to feel as though he wanted to face the distant window and bow to the Blood Heaven Saber Demon wherever he might be.

That day, Seo Daeryong could not fall asleep until late, lost in one thought after another.

* * *

The next morning, we ate breakfast and headed for the Martial Alliance.

In the distance, the magnificent buildings of the Martial Alliance’s main headquarters began to appear. A gigantic stone statue of a martial artist modeled after a chivalrous hero stood before us.

The closer we drew to the Martial Alliance, the stranger I felt. Someday, the Martial Alliance would also be swept away by Hwa Mugi.

Would their fate change because I had regressed? Or would only our church’s fate change? I still did not know.

Seo Daeryong kept sneaking glances at the passing female heroes.

“Orthodox women are beautiful, aren’t they?”

“They certainly are. I thought it was only a rumor.”

“Since we’re here, try dating one. The fated love between an orthodox female hero and a demon!”

“It would be fun for the spectators. But how difficult would it be for the people involved?”

He spoke lightly, but his gaze kept drifting toward the women passing by.

A separate registration area for the Little Dragon Tournament had been prepared in the outer grounds of the Martial Alliance.

Even though registration was open for nearly five days, the line was still long. Countless young talents from orthodox sects across the Central Plains had flocked here.

We joined the line.

As we waited, the martial artists ahead of us began discussing the tournament.

To summarize their conversation, it seemed that they would not select only the champion. Everyone who made it into the final eight would also be chosen and placed in the Martial Alliance’s elite organization.

One of the men chatting glanced at Seo Daeryong and me, then smirked. Seeing our country-bumpkin appearance, he seemed to have instinctively looked down on us.

Loud enough for him to hear, I asked Seo Daeryong,

“Young Master, are you confident you can win?”

Seo Daeryong’s eyes issued a powerful protest—*Win? All of a sudden?*—but his answer was forceful.

“I am.”

The men ahead exchanged glances and snickered. Still, they did not openly pick a fight or ignore us.

At last, after waiting for quite some time, it was our turn.

“Name: Seoryong. Sect: the Western Saber Sect of Gansu.”

It was an actual sect in Gansu, directly operated by the Tongtian Pavilion for situations like this. If anyone went to investigate, they would find a son named Seoryong, who had supposedly left to participate in the Little Dragon Tournament.

“The tournament brackets will be posted in front of the Martial Alliance in ten days. Check the date and dueling platform then. If you are late for your match, you will be eliminated without exception, so arrive early and wait.”

“Understood.”

After completing our registration, we left the Martial Alliance.

“You saw how those bastards looked down on us earlier, right? Since they gave me the idea, maybe I should just go ahead and win the whole thing.”

“It’s certainly possible.”

“Can I really do it? I may not look it, but I’ve been taking first place in everything since I was young. Don’t underestimate me!”

“I’m not underestimating you. Do it. You may win.”

“I really will!”

And from that very day, Seo Daeryong trained in the mountains until the tournament began. I did not help him. He was learning from the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, so it was not my place to interfere. I simply trusted Doma’s teachings.

Whenever Seo Daeryong went out to train, I trained as well. Of course, I went somewhere else so that I would not disturb him.

I had trained tremendously hard in the mountains even before Seo Daeryong arrived, but this time I trained even harder. And this training rewarded me with the finest result.

“I did it!”

At last, the Wind God Four Steps had reached the ninth stage.

Honestly, I was happier than when I had achieved perfection in the twelve stages of the Flying Heaven Sword Technique.

My Flickering Step could now escape dangerous situations even more quickly, my Shadow Step could evade people’s eyes like an illusion, and my Underworld King Step had become so powerful that I could confidently call it devastatingly lethal.

And my Swift Step had become terrifyingly fast. Its internal energy consumption had decreased, but the expenditure of mental strength had doubled. It was simply too fast. At first, I even crashed into a tree. If not for the Divine Eye Art, it would have been impossible to control that speed even across a wide plain.

Once I grew accustomed to this ninth-stage realm, the Wind God Four Steps, with only its final hurdle remaining, would say this to me:

—Amazing, isn’t it? This is what the ninth stage is like. What do you think it’ll be like once you reach perfection? Come on, let’s put in just a little more effort and get there!

I had to hear those words. Only then could I say this to the Wind God Four Steps after achieving perfection:

—You worked hard too!

I would definitely say those words.

After that, I would inherit the Nine Flames Demonic Art and achieve perfection in it as well.

Even after perfecting both martial arts, I would continue working hard without rest.

If I still could not stand against Hwa Mugi after going this far… then there would be nothing I could do. At that point, I would gladly smile and die.

* * *

The tournament brackets were announced.

My first match was the thirteenth bout of the Yellow Group, to be held the next day. So many people had gathered that the matches would be held simultaneously at dozens of locations in the outer grounds of the Martial Alliance.

“If our church held the same tournament, would this many people come?”

“They wouldn’t.”

“This is the power of the orthodox sects. For one thing, they probably have ten times our numbers.”

And that was not all. The orthodox sects also had vastly more reclusive masters.

That was why we could not rashly start a war simply because we were stronger than the Martial Alliance at present. Even if we drove them back and swept all the way to the Martial Alliance’s main headquarters, the real war would only begin from that day onward.

As Seo Daeryong and I ate and chatted about this and that, the waiter hurried over to us.

“I’m sorry, but would you mind sharing your table? So many customers have come because of the Little Dragon Tournament.”

“Of course.”

“Thank you.”

Soon afterward, a woman wearing a bamboo hat joined us.

The moment she sat down and removed her hat, I was startled. She was someone I knew.

She was a woman whose status made it seem impossible that she would ever share a table at an inn, whose cold yet intelligent beauty had earned her the title of Hubei’s Number One Beauty, and who possessed outstanding martial prowess as well.

Amazingly, she was none other than Jin Haryeong, the granddaughter of the Martial Alliance Lord.

Chapter 155: The More One Becomes a Master, the Hotter the Desire Grows.
------------------------------------------------------------------------
Jin Ha-ryeong ordered noodles and wine.

She had come alone, yet for some reason she ordered two bowls of noodles.

Her expressionless face and low voice made people tense; neither the waiter taking her order nor Seo Daeryong sitting across from her could properly meet her eyes.

And she had a knack for finding people who were not intimidated by her.

Our eyes met, and a faint unusual light flickered through hers. It must have been rare for someone to look at her face so calmly.

But soon she turned her gaze elsewhere.

Seo Daeryong’s sound transmission flew to me.

—She’s truly beautiful. Women of the orthodox sects really are far too beautiful.

Seo Daeryong’s voice was flushed with excitement.

Though she must have felt Seo Daeryong stealing glances at her, she paid him no mind at all, as if it were a familiar part of daily life.

Before long, the wine and two bowls of noodles came out.

As she was about to pour herself a drink, Seo Daeryong mustered his courage.

“Allow me to pour you a cup.”

Jin Ha-ryeong stared fixedly at Seo Daeryong.

“I don’t mean anything by it. It’s just that sitting together at the same table is a kind of fate.”

But Jin Ha-ryeong silently poured and drank by herself.

A sound transmission filled with self-reproach, as though the world were collapsing, flew to me.

—Ah, that was far too trite, wasn’t it? Sitting together at the same table is fate! Ah! Even my grandfather wouldn’t have said something like that.

Just then, setting down her wine cup, Jin Ha-ryeong asked me,

“Are you participating in the Little Dragon Tournament?”

I looked at her for a moment, then glanced at Seo Daeryong and said,

“Our young master is participating.”

“Young master?”

“Yes. I am the servant who attends upon him.”

With an unexpected look, Jin Ha-ryeong looked back and forth between me and Seo Daeryong. As if her eyes were saying, Aren’t you the young master and that one the servant? Seo Daeryong, for no reason, began scolding me.

“It’s because this young brat grew a beard. You rascal! Isn’t the young lady confused because of that scruffy beard of yours?”

“My apologies, young master.”

Soon, as if she had no interest in any of this, she began eating her noodles.

Seo Daeryong’s sound transmission flew to me.

—Now that I look again, she’s not that great. This woman.

Seeing Seo Daeryong pout, I laughed inwardly.

Just then, a man in his early thirties entered the inn. Judging by the aura he revealed and the way he walked, he was quite an expert, and he seemed to be Jin Ha-ryeong’s bodyguard.

“I’ve been searching for you for quite some time.”

“Sit.”

“You must go now.”

“Sit first. We’re not the only ones here, are we?”

The man quickly looked us over, then immediately sat down beside her.

She pushed a bowl of noodles toward him.

“Eat. The noodles here are good.”

She had ordered two bowls of noodles knowing this man would come looking for her.

“I’m fine.”

“I’m not fine. Hurry and eat.”

“Yes.”

The man began eating the noodles. He slurped down the noodles, then quickly finished even the broth.

If Ian had been here, he would have said he understood that man. Bodyguards, by necessity, got into the habit of eating that quickly.

Then Jin Ha-ryeong said to him,

“If you eat that fast, you’ll ruin your insides and die before you get cut down by a blade.”

“I am fine.”

“You’re fine. It’s always me who isn’t fine.”

The man rose from his seat and urged her on.

“You must go now. They will be looking for you, miss.”

“Yes, I should go.”

Jin Ha-ryeong emptied her wine, rose from her seat, and left with the man.

When she disappeared into the distance, Seo Daeryong said with a wistful look in his eyes,

“She came like fate, then left after eating only noodles.”

“You said she wasn’t that great when you looked again.”

“My heart is narrow, that’s all. It’s not that the woman is lacking. She’s the prettiest woman I’ve seen since coming here.”

“That woman is impossible.”

“Why? You mean I, who look like a servant, am impossible? Is that it?”

“Even I, this dashing man who can’t hide the fact that I’m a young master no matter how much of a beard I grow, am impossible.”

“Why you, Pavilion Master?”

“Because a romance between the son of the Heavenly Demon and the granddaughter of the Martial Alliance Leader would be far too fated a love.”

At the words “granddaughter of the Martial Alliance Leader,” Seo Daeryong was startled.

“She’s the Martial Alliance Leader’s granddaughter?”

“Yes.”

“Why would the Martial Alliance Leader’s granddaughter eat noodles in an inn like this? And even share a table?”

“How would I know? Maybe she got sick of a banquet hall filled with expensive and rare dishes and ran out. Or maybe she just wanted to eat this place’s noodles.”

“Ah! Life truly is unpredictable. Who would have known that I’d end up sharing a table with you and the Martial Alliance Leader’s granddaughter at an inn in front of the Martial Alliance?”

“Is that all? You’re going to have a martial duel with that Martial Alliance Leader’s granddaughter too.”

Seo Daeryong was even more shocked.

“What do you mean?”

“She’ll be entering the Little Dragon Tournament as well.”

Seo Daeryong’s eyes went wide.

That was the reason I remembered this Little Dragon Tournament. It was the tournament that made her name known to everyone.

The winner of this tournament had been none other than Jin Ha-ryeong.

At the time, I had simply accepted it and moved on. It wasn’t particularly surprising for the Martial Alliance Leader’s granddaughter to win a martial tournament for rising talents.

But now I knew. Behind this tournament was the Cheonmyeong Society.

The reason Jin Ha-ryeong’s fate later flowed in a direction that shocked the world was that this tournament had been the starting point.

“Win, Inspector Seo.”

Seo Daeryong, realizing that I was speaking sincerely, drank the wine set before him.

“You’re saying something I can’t answer while sober.”

* * *

The next day was Seo Daeryong’s first martial duel.

The outer court of the Martial Alliance had been arranged with dozens of stages, and each stage was crowded with participants and spectators.

Yet despite the large number of people, the duels proceeded in an orderly fashion without a hitch.

There was a certain pressure that the Martial Alliance gave off. In addition, the warriors overseeing the tournament were from one of the Martial Alliance’s elite organizations, the Azure Dragon Corps, so there was no one picking fights or causing disturbances.

The warrior managing the Yellow-character group to which Seo Daeryong belonged came and informed them of the precautions.

“If you deliberately inflict serious injury on or kill your opponent, you will be disqualified without exception. Also, you may be held accountable for your crime and imprisoned in the dungeon, so be careful. This match is a proper martial duel, not a fight to the death.”

His first opponent was a warrior from a sword sect in Hubei. He had a fairly solid build and sharp eyes.

“I’m so nervous I could die.”

“Were you this nervous when you took the promotion exam for inspector at Hwangcheon Pavilion?”

“I wasn’t nervous at all then.”

“It’s the same, isn’t it? The effort you put in.”

Seo Daeryong opened his eyes wide and looked at me.

“I know better than anyone that you trained hard. That’s why you’re here. If that weren’t the case, I would have grown my beard longer and gone up there myself.”

Perhaps my words gave him strength, because Seo Daeryong nodded vigorously.

“I’ll go win.”

Seo Daeryong knocked his opponent down and came back down in just three exchanges. He even won without drawing his saber.

“That was too easy.”

“Don’t forget whose disciple you are.”

Seo Daeryong had finally gained an opportunity to view his own skill objectively. He did not yet know how important that was for Seo Daeryong the martial artist.

Just then, the warrior overseeing the duel came and informed him,

“There will be one more duel this afternoon, so do not go anywhere and wait.”

Since it was still the early stage of the tournament, it seemed they were holding duels twice a day.

“So if you get injured in the morning duel, you have to fight the afternoon duel while injured. They’re testing our fighting spirit too, aren’t they?”

“What fighting spirit? It’s probably because their schedule is tight.”

“Don’t look down on the fighting spirit of our orthodox sects!”

“Yes, young master! Let your fighting spirit burn!”

Seo Daeryong won his afternoon duel easily as well. This time too, he won without even using his saber.

“How does it feel to win both matches on the first day?”

“It feels good.”

Though Seo Daeryong had trained diligently under the Blood Heaven Saber Demon until now, he had never fought martial duels like this in actual practice.

“I was very nervous before going up, but once I actually stood on the stage, I wasn’t that nervous. It was a strange experience.”

“I told you it would be a good experience.”

“I’ve never regretted listening to you, Pavilion Master.”

“Go back first and rest.”

“What about you, Pavilion Master?”

“I’ll look around a bit before going.”

“Yes, then I’ll go ahead.”

After sending Seo Daeryong ahead, I walked around the dueling stages.

Duels were still in full swing on every stage.

Then I saw that one stage in particular was unusually crowded with spectators. When I went over, Jin Ha-ryeong, whom I had seen yesterday, was on the stage.

She was the Martial Alliance Leader’s granddaughter and a beauty called the finest in Hubei, so she was truly at the center of attention. The spectators were exchanging stories about her.

“But why did the Alliance Leader’s granddaughter enter this tournament?”

“It means they won’t simply hand her a position just because she’s his granddaughter. If she wants to join the Martial Alliance, she has to enter proudly through skill, doesn’t it?”

“That’s right, that’s right. Didn’t Miss Jin’s older brother win the tournament a few years ago and enter the Martial Alliance too?”

“As expected! Our Alliance Leader is a fair and impartial man.”

Jin Ha-ryeong also subdued her opponent without drawing her sword.

Receiving cheers as she stepped down from the stage, she left without giving a single glance to anyone. At that aloof appearance, the crowd grew even more excited.

After watching a few more duels, I returned to the inn. Seo Daeryong had gone to the mountains to train and came back late at night. Though he had won easily today, it seemed he was concerned about the remaining duels ahead.

“As I told you, I’m the sort who can’t stand not taking first place. If I don’t enter, that’s one thing, but if I do, I have to do my best.”

Seo Daeryong’s grit could not have appeared from nowhere. It must have been a character forged through efforts like this.

Seo Daeryong won his match the next day as well.

“You’re saying you didn’t even watch my match?”

“There was an interesting duel on another stage.”

“Who was it? Don’t tell me it was the Martial Alliance Leader’s granddaughter.”

“That’s right.”

This time as well, I watched Jin Ha-ryeong’s duel. Judging by Seo Daeryong’s ability, he might have to face her. Of course, since they were in different groups, it would not be immediately, but if he became his group’s winner and advanced to the main tournament, he could end up facing her.

And there was an even more important reason I was paying attention to her. She was both the target and the center of the scheme the Cheonmyeong Society was plotting this time. She would surely become the path that connected me to them.

“What if I’d met a strong opponent while you were gone?”

“You think I just left? I checked who your opponent was before I went. You won by attacking the opening on his left, didn’t you?”

“Huh? How did you know?”

“From the way he walked.”

I appeased the amazed Seo Daeryong’s mood. This was the moment when I ought to be admiring him.

“Does winning feel good?”

“Honestly, it feels amazing!”

“Well done.”

Seo Daeryong could not hide the joy on his face.

“Do you know when it feels best? Surprisingly, it wasn’t the moment I knocked down my opponent. It felt best when the duel was over and I stepped down from the stage. That moment when people looked at me, cheered, and applauded was more thrilling and exciting. This time, I learned that I’m someone who’s very conscious of other people’s eyes.”

“The stronger you become, the more you’ll want them to watch you.”

“There’s no way.”

“Why? Do you think you’ll become more dignified? More humble?”

“Is that not the case?”

“Let’s suppose someone becomes the greatest under heaven. The moment he becomes the greatest under heaven, will he be happy because he defeated the previous greatest under heaven? Or will he be happy because from that day on, everyone respects and looks up to him as the greatest under heaven?”

Imagine going somewhere and hearing someone shout, “The greatest under heaven is arriving!” Imagine every person you meet looking at you with eyes full of respect and envy. Imagine the drums of victory always sounding, applause and cheers always ringing out.

“People’s hearts are all the same. No, perhaps the stronger one becomes, the hotter and more primal that desire becomes. The heart of the greatest under heaven is broader? Is that really so? I’m the greatest under heaven, and they dare treat me like this? Are you confident you won’t fall into that trap? Are you confident you can keep that pettiness buried to the end, when you no longer have to hide it?”

After a brief pause, Seo Daeryong said,

“But you aren’t like that, Pavilion Master, are you? You won’t be like that, right?”

“Why do you think I’m different? Am I not human? I’m the same. I’m simply trying not to be.”

“It feels so good to have this kind of conversation with you, Pavilion Master. I finally feel as though I’ve become a true martial artist. I can’t remember the last time my heart pounded like this. I sincerely apologize for insisting that I wouldn’t enter this tournament.”

But this feeling of our Seo Daeryong changed immediately the very next day.

“I almost died! I’m not exaggerating—I really almost died! Ugh, I told you I didn’t want to do this! I want to go back!”

“It’s just a scratch. You’re fine.”

“A scratch? Can’t you see all this blood? Ah, seriously, if I hadn’t dodged by a hair’s breadth, I’d have a hole this big in my shoulder! And his sword was enormous, too.”

“Your opponent can’t even get up right now.”

The opponent who had drawn blood from Seo Daeryong’s shoulder was still unconscious, unable to come to his senses.

“I don’t care. Please send me home! I have to go water my flowerpots!”

Seo Daeryong, who had been like that, was in high spirits again the next day.

Seo Daeryong was receiving the kind of attention he had wanted even below the sparring stage.
